DeusData/codebase-memory-mcp ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v0.12.1 Stable2026-10-16T23:35:48Z

v0.12.1

Every install path works again, and Helm charts join the graph.

v0.12.1 fixes v0.12.0's package installs and the other install paths we found broken while checking every channel. It also links Helm charts to what they deploy, and stops the graph from inventing a registry for container images.

Upgrading? The first index of each existing project after the upgrade is a full rebuild, once (image identities changed; see below).

Fixed: installs

  • npm, PyPI and Go installs. npm i, pnpm add, pip install and the Go wrapper could not install v0.12.0 on any platform. Each wrapper refused an archive member above 256 MiB, and every v0.12.0 binary is 331-335 MB. The size limits are gone from all three wrappers. The archive is still verified against the release's checksums.txt before it is unpacked, its contents must be exactly the expected files, and the binary must answer --version. Thank you, @hydroplexus, for the precise report (#2657).
  • Homebrew. The formula was still at 0.10.3, and on Linux it installed the dynamically linked build, which needs glibc 2.38 (Ubuntu 22.04, Debian 12 and RHEL 9 do not have it). It now installs the current release, and on Linux the static -portable build that runs on any distribution.
  • go install. A pinned go install .../pkg/go/cmd/codebase-memory-mcp@vX.Y.Z never worked, and @latest installed a stale version. Releases now carry pkg/go/vX.Y.Z tags, and the wrapper installs the release matching its own version.
  • npm next. The next dist-tag no longer points to an old release candidate; a stable release moves it.
  • Docs. winget and Chocolatey were listed as install channels but are not published; they are removed until they are. Windows arm64 builds ship and are now listed.

Install-path smoke tests. Every pull request and every release now installs the npm, PyPI and Go wrappers, the way a user's package manager does, against the real release archives, and runs the binary each one installed. A release is published only after these installs pass, so an installer that cannot install what we ship can no longer reach you.

Helm charts in the graph

A Helm chart used to be an isolated node. Now it links to what it deploys (#2663):

  • Chart → objects (DEPLOYS, via: chart): every Kubernetes object rendered from the chart's templates/ (a subchart's objects belong to the subchart).
  • values file → Chart (CONFIGURES): values.yaml and values-<x>.yaml beside Chart.yaml.
  • Chart → images (DEPLOYS, ref: image): every image its objects name literally (a templated image names none).

HelmRelease → Chart → Deployment → Image is now one traversable path. On 20 infrastructure repositories, charts with no outgoing edge dropped from 238 of 260 to 109, and 2,511 chart-to-object links appeared; every other edge stayed identical.

Images keep the registry the code names

An image without a registry host used to be stored under an invented docker.io/library/... (#2654). Docker Hub is only Docker's default: Podman and CRI-O resolve short names through a search list, mirrors rewrite them, and a local build tag may exist on no registry at all. An image now keeps its name as written, with registry: unknown when no host is stated; a stated host is kept (Docker Hub's spellings become docker.io). FROM nginx and image: docker.io/library/nginx are now two Image nodes. This changes image identities, which is why each project is rebuilt once after the upgrade.

Upgrade notes

  • The first index of each existing project after the upgrade is a full rebuild, once.
  • Image nodes without a stated registry now carry registry: unknown and the name as written, instead of docker.io/library/....

<!-- cbm-security-verification:start -->

@oh-my-pi/pi-ai

Fixed

  • Fixed JSON Schema dereferencing rewriting literal values in const, enum, default, examples, and custom annotations, or treating property names as schema keywords (#15927 by @jchanghong023).
  • Fixed Anthropic requests hanging forever on Bun 1.3 when the upstream accepted the request but never sent headers: aborting the request (including the first-event timeout) now fails it, so the turn retries instead of waiting until the user interrupts it (#16010 by @ca-io-systems)
  • Fixed the auth gateway's POST /v1/chat/completions streams being dropped (terminated) after ~255 s without upstream output, e.g. while xAI generates a large tool call's arguments, which arrive only when complete; the stream now sends an SSE : keepalive comment every 15 s (#15940 by @radkawar)
  • Fixed Anthropic models with mid-conversation system messages (e.g. Claude Opus 5.5) receiving @file mention contents and hidden extension messages as a trailing system message instead of ending the request on the user's turn (#15881)

@oh-my-pi/pi-catalog

Changed

  • Renamed the built-in google-antigravity login option to "Google Antigravity (Gemini, Claude, GPT-OSS)" so it is distinguishable from similarly named third-party plugins and no longer implies support stops at Gemini 3 (#15998 by @savanne-kham).

Fixed

  • Fixed Command Code's z-ai/glm-5.3-flash failing every request with "not available on this endpoint": the catalog routes it to chat completions, the only endpoint Command Code lists for it (#15922 by @jasonharrison)
  • Fixed /handoff failing with 400 Provider returned error on OpenRouter's hosted Muse Spark 1.x and Muse Wonder models: requests no longer send a tool_choice the upstream Meta backend rejects (#15910)
  • Fixed OpenCode Zen bringing back retired bundled models from an authoritative model cache, and dropping drop-listed models such as gemini-3.7-flash when a refresh fails after an upgrade (#15770 by @adi-IL)

@oh-my-pi/pi-coding-agent

Breaking Changes

  • Extension ui.approve() hosts receive an optional session choice label and answer "once", "session", or "deny" instead of a boolean

Added

  • Added imageCountBudget to custom models and modelOverrides in models.yml, so a custom gateway can state its host's image limit instead of the default of 5; past that limit the oldest images were dropped on every new image, which rewrote the provider's prompt cache (#15002, #15969 by @aramalipoor)
  • The status line names the active profile next to the π brand (π-work), so sessions from different profiles are distinguishable at a glance; the default profile is unchanged (#15622 by @matthewrball)
  • Added opt-in prewalk.auto (off, suggest, or on): the native judge decides per task whether to arm a prewalk handoff to prewalk.autoTarget, and the next task starts back on the planning model (#16026 by @taibenvenuti)
  • Added /seance and the task seance agent: consult a saved session read-only, in place, without resuming it or copying its artifacts; pick the source and an optional model from pickers, and send follow-ups to the consultant (#15036 by @anatoli-tsinovoy)

Changed

  • Tool approval prompts list Approve, Approve <tool> for this session, and Deny again: with an empty composer, pick with the arrow keys and Enter; typing in the composer denies and steers instead, and the Deny row reads "Deny and send message" while you do

Fixed

  • Fixed the Isolate Subagents setting description implying that all tasks run isolated; each task must opt in with isolated: true (#15987).
  • Fixed read labeling re-encoded images with the display format instead of the source file's format; it now identifies both when they differ (#15890).
  • Fixed omp -p and task subagents reporting a clean finish with an earlier answer when a context overflow could not be compacted enough to retry; the overflow error is now the run's outcome (#15897 by @nhthung)
  • Fixed Cursor's Shell tool killing commands once its block_until_ms wait elapsed; the wait no longer sets the kill deadline, which comes from Cursor's hard_timeout or the bash default (#15898 by @nhthung)
  • Fixed an interrupted prompt's late setup cleanup marking the next prompt idle early, allowing overlapping submissions and releasing sleep prevention mid-turn (#15946)
  • Fixed hashline REM hiding the path-recovery warning when a nonexistent path matched an earlier-read file (#15957).
  • Fixed tool downloads (sd, sg, yt-dlp, trafilatura) failing with GitHub API error: 403 on a machine whose egress IP has spent GitHub's anonymous 60/hour budget; the release lookup now sends GITHUB_TOKEN/GH_TOKEN like omp's other GitHub API call sites (#15981 by @iacore)
  • Background local memory extraction now stops after one bounded retry budget on repeated OpenAI-compatible 429 responses instead of multiplying transport, provider, and completion retries (#15826).
  • Fixed a message-result subagent resumed by typing into its focused session leaving agent://<id> empty and never reporting its final reply to the parent (#15948)
  • Fixed read of an RSS or Atom feed showing Untitled for every item and dropping feed titles, descriptions, dates, and RSS item links (#15954)
  • Fixed read showing a binary notice instead of an HTML page when a server sends unlabeled gzip to the first request but decodable HTML to the second (#15956)
  • Fixed read returning a homepage's site-wide feed or a page's site-wide llms.txt alternate instead of the requested page (#15955).
  • Fixed ast_grep reporting a clean "No matches found" for a pattern that does not parse in the target language (e.g. a bare C# identifier); it now lists the pattern's ERROR node as a parse issue (#15950)
  • Fixed --resume <id> and session listings skipping transcripts placed in the session directory as symlinks (#15878, #15884 by @peda-cos)
  • Fixed sessions with a deferred-connecting MCP server listing every routed MCP tool twice, once as a mount notice and once in route guidance (#15982 by @zcyoung2000)

Removed

  • Removed the built-in rs-parking-lot rule; Rust edits using std::sync::{Mutex, RwLock} with .lock().unwrap() are no longer steered to parking_lot

@oh-my-pi/pi-natives

Changed

  • Changed encodeSixel and encodeSixelAsync to drop a partial last 6-pixel band, so an image never extends below the requested height and the caller no longer has to shrink it to a multiple of 6 (#16007)

@oh-my-pi/pi-tui

Added

  • Added selection-only session and model picker actions for /seance, so choosing a source or model leaves the active session and model unchanged (#15036 by @anatoli-tsinovoy)

Changed

  • ApprovalDialogComponent shows a choice list (Approve, an optional session row, Deny): the arrow keys move between them and Enter (armed after APPROVE_ARM_MS) takes the highlighted one; while a composer draft holds Enter the highlight sits on Deny, relabeled "Deny and send message"; Tern clicks on a choice take it; Page Up/Down scroll the details

Fixed

  • Fixed a streamed Markdown list keeping every item out of native scrollback until the turn ended, so rows that scrolled off screen were unreachable mid-stream; items in front of the newest one now retire as soon as it begins (#13676, #15993 by @shgew)
  • Fixed SIXEL charts and SVG figures being resampled before display, which softened their text, on terminals whose cell height is not a multiple of 6 such as Windows Terminal (#16007)

@oh-my-pi/pi-utils

Fixed

  • Fixed Markdown fence info-string parsing to keep backtick-containing backtick openers as paragraph text while accepting backticks in tilde info strings and Unicode line separators in either fence's info string (#15928 by @jchanghong023).
  • Fixed parseHTML treating <link> in RSS/Atom documents as an HTML void element, which dropped the URL text of RSS <link>…</link> (#15954)

v18.11.2 Stable2026-10-16T15:05:57Z

@oh-my-pi/pi-agent-core

Added

  • agent_start carries resumedToolCallIds when a continued run first executes the calls of an unpaired assistant tail

Fixed

  • Cancelling an ask after a background job finishes now reports the user cancellation instead of telling the model to retry a skipped tool (#15985)

@oh-my-pi/pi-ai

Breaking Changes

  • resolveGatewayApiKey and buildGatewayApiKeyResolver (@oh-my-pi/pi-ai/auth-gateway) take the request's route options ({ storage, client? }) instead of an AuthStorage, their resolver callback receives the resolved credential, and resolveGatewayAccount takes that credential (#15900 by @alphastorm)

Added

  • Added scoped clients to startAuthGateway (clients, setClients): each client bearer is limited to its own models and OAuth accounts, with no fallback to other accounts or API keys, and request log lines name the client and the credential a request started on (#5098, #15900 by @alphastorm)
  • Added experimental native image judgments for TypeSafe Jev's jev-latest, jev-preview, jev-1.13.0, and jev-1.14.0, plus OpenRouter's typesafe/jev-1.13, typesafe/jev-1.14, and ~typesafe/jev-latest models; images outside Jev's documented size bounds fall through to a vision chat judge instead of failing (#15887 by @anatoli-tsinovoy)

Fixed

  • Fixed error results from non-first-party providers regaining partial output the provider had discarded; an error with empty content now stays empty (#14516, #14572 by @oleg494)
  • Fixed the auth gateway's POST /v1/audio/speech answering 502 for requests the speech adapter rejects (unsupported format, over-long input); they now get 400 invalid_request_error (#14508 by @F0Rextasy)
  • Fixed a single-string tool call with two or more mislabelled string fields silently dispatching whichever field came first in the JSON; such ambiguous calls now fail validation and the model sees its own arguments back (#14414 by @F0Rextasy)
  • Fixed GLM-5.2 on Ollama Cloud losing its reasoning between turns; Ollama Cloud now keeps assistant thinking in history for models verified to accept it (#14440 by @Dante-dan)
  • Fixed GPT Image generation and edits failing on gateways that validate request parameters, such as LiteLLM; response_format is no longer sent to GPT Image models (#14619 by @jimhester)
  • Fixed OpenRouter errors such as "Access denied by security policy" dropping the routed upstream's own explanation from error.metadata.raw (#10906, #14435 by @F0Rextasy)
  • Fixed GitHub Copilot usage reporting an AI-credit allowance as "Premium Requests" in request units; the unit each billing item reports is now honoured (#13849, #14501 by @F0Rextasy)
  • Fixed an OpenAI-compatible server's finish_reason: "repetition" or stop_reason: "repetition_detected" not being treated as a thinking loop, so the degenerate partial reasoning was kept and replayed into the next turn (#12525, #14535 by @F0Rextasy)

@oh-my-pi/pi-catalog

Added

  • Added keep-bundled-names catalog attribute to preserve static model display names during discovery
  • Updated openai-codex provider configuration to prevent model name regressions during live discovery

Changed

  • GPT Image models (gpt-image-*, chatgpt-image-*) now classify as their own gpt-image family instead of gpt or chatgpt, so GPT chat-model rules no longer apply to them (#14619 by @jimhester)

Fixed

  • Fixed OpenCode Zen Gemini 4 Argon failing with an unsupported protocol; it now uses the native Gemini endpoint (#15977).

@oh-my-pi/pi-coding-agent

Added

  • Added lsp.requestTimeout setting to configure wait durations for language server diagnostics and requests
  • Added omp auth-gateway serve --clients=<file>: per-client bearer tokens, each limited to listed models and broker OAuth accounts with no fallback to other accounts or API keys, re-read while serving (#5098, #15900 by @alphastorm)

Changed

  • The lsp tool now uses lsp.requestTimeout to dynamically adjust both call deadlines and project-aware diagnostic wait times, with support for per-call overrides
  • Reduced CPU overhead during syntax checks and automatic edit repairs (#15904 by @iliaal).
  • Reduced status-line refresh overhead while subagents have long conversations (#15874 by @iliaal).
  • Agents now send todo updates alongside their next tool call instead of spending a separate model turn on them (#15402 by @will-bogusz)
  • The Shared Language Servers setting now says what it does: each connection gets its own server, and a released one is reused while another omp instance keeps the project's mux running (#14633 by @jorgoose)
  • Memories saved explicitly through the extension memory.save API are now stored as stated rather than unknown, so they rank as deliberate memories in Mnemopi recall (#13824 by @sjawhar)

Fixed

  • Fixed prewalk switching models during read-only MCP lookups through write xd:// when the server marks the tool read-only (#15994)
  • Fixed Ctrl+C printing 9;5u in the shell after force-quitting omp with a third Ctrl+C while it was still closing the session
  • Fixed Smart unexpected-stop recovery continuing finished answers, which added extra "nothing more to add" turns
  • Fixed grep searching only the first 4 MB of a file named directly, so later matches were reported as absent; the whole file is now searched, and a multiline pattern, which still reads only the first 4 MB, says so in its no-match headline (#15942)
  • Fixed browser uploadFile failing with "Not allowed" through the relay or into a Chrome on another machine, stalling behind macOS folder-privacy prompts, and breaking the tab on large drop-zone files (#15489 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed browser uploadFile sending a stale copy of a file rewritten without changing its size or timestamp, failing on folders that contain a symlink loop, and deleting copies a still-open attached page could read after a repeat upload
  • Fixed plan mode (including plan-yolo) looping on write xd://propose forever when an extension provides a tool named write; the extension tool is left out of plan mode with a warning, since only the built-in write can submit plans (#14030, #14438 by @F0Rextasy)
  • Fixed legacy Mnemopi bank databases staying open after the recall bank probe, which could block deleting them on Windows (#14584 by @yshihao636)
  • Fixed startup skipping a configured default fallback whose provider authenticates only through another provider's key (e.g. xai-oauth via XAI_API_KEY) and picking an unrelated model instead (#15781)
  • Fixed slower startup when the configured default model is unavailable and no fallback chain is configured (#15781)
  • Fixed read directory listings printing a directory's raw listing error, whose tabs, newlines, or control characters could break the transcript; [unreadable: …] is now a single bounded line (#15789)
  • Fixed a ^ model mention sent while the agent is working (steer, follow-up, or queued skill prompt) never announcing its new m<N> alias to the model (#14093, #14322 by @holny)
  • Fixed large session files ending in a truncated record not being repaired on load, unlike small ones (#14582 by @yshihao636)
  • Fixed a read-only subagent's rejected write reading as a dead end: when yield is available, the rejection now names it as the way to return the content (#14308, #14470 by @F0Rextasy)
  • Fixed /retry after an interrupt leaving later advisor notes and extension asides unable to wake the agent until the next typed prompt (#14831 by @daandden)
  • Fixed session titles and idle recaps disappearing after a corrupt history.db was quarantined and recreated, and a briefly busy store disabling the session index for the rest of the session (#13929, #14361 by @F0Rextasy)
  • Fixed pasting a screenshot with ⌘V into omp running over SSH from Tern or kitty failing with Clipboard is empty or Image not found (#15369 by @LoneExile)
  • Fixed native hook discovery silently ignoring hooks symlinked into hooks/pre or hooks/post (#14189, #14536 by @F0Rextasy)
  • Fixed headless Linux containers without a system D-Bus logging a Failed to acquire power assertion warning on every turn; the missing backend is now noted once at debug level (#14591 by @tahakotil)
  • Fixed a project's daemon broker staying unreachable after its runtime directory was deleted while it ran (proc://, wait, shared LSP and the shared browser failed with connect ENOENT …/broker.sock); the broker now releases the project within seconds of losing its socket and the next request starts a fresh one (#14466 by @nick-maderight)
  • Fixed SQL and Redis sessions stalling after a rewrite raced an append; later turns now republish after the backend recovers (#15612 by @sjawhar)
  • Fixed OpenAI Codex web search failing with "No Codex OAuth credentials found" when only OPENAI_CODEX_OAUTH_TOKEN is set (#12537, #14337 by @F0Rextasy)
  • Fixed yield rejecting a valid data result when a non-strict provider also filled error with a placeholder such as unused or n/a; a real error alongside data is still rejected, now with exact resend instructions (#15633 by @Komzpa)
  • Fixed secret redaction replaying Gemini tool calls with their original thoughtSignature after rewriting the call arguments; a rewritten call now drops the signature, as rewritten thinking already did (#14392 by @F0Rextasy)
  • Fixed Claude marketplace plugin MCP servers declaring a sub-second timeout (e.g. SAP's "timeout": 600) never loading; values below Claude Code's 1000 ms minimum are now ignored with a warning so the default timeout applies (#12485, #14574 by @oleg494)
  • Fixed automatic retries looping without end when the primary model's cooldown expired mid-retry: the session no longer switches back to the primary while a retry is still in flight, and returns to it on the next prompt (#14611 by @MiloMMIN)
  • Fixed web search providers garbling non-UTF-8 responses; they now decode with the charset declared in Content-Type (or a <meta charset> tag), the same way fetched pages are (#15304 by @yuzu-octopus)
  • Fixed openai-models-list discovery ignoring a flat max_tokens output limit on /v1/models rows, which pinned such models to the 32K default (#13062, #14403 by @F0Rextasy)
  • Fixed long replies dropping lines from scrollback or replaying them duplicated and out of order when status widgets stay taller after a turn or transient HUDs close (#15820 by @emmahyde)
  • Fixed a stale "Working…" row staying under a focused subagent after it finished while the main session kept running (#15820)
  • Fixed omp update failing when a bunfig minimumReleaseAge or npmrc min-release-age supply-chain cooldown rejects the just-published release; self-update now overrides the cooldown for the pinned version it installs (#14581 by @morri-son)
  • Fixed agent://<id> returning a message-result subagent's first result after it completed a parent-requested follow-up; reads now return its latest completed result (#15707 by @mg-mg-mg)
  • Fixed sloppy edits silently dropping an operator when one *** Find was followed by two (e.g. *** Insert Before and *** Replace); the payload is now rejected with an error naming both and nothing is applied (#14357 by @F0Rextasy)
  • Fixed a cancelled Python tool install (e.g. uv tool install) falling through to pip with the aborted signal and leaving the install progress running (#15750 by @harshaygadekar)
  • Fixed auto-learn captures rewriting earlier OpenAI Responses history after a learning tool call, which broke prompt-cache reuse (#14099 by @nanbloom001)
  • Fixed /shake images leaving the context percentage unchanged; the tokens freed by the removed images are now reported and subtracted from the context meter right away (#5029, #14367 by @F0Rextasy)
  • Fixed /fork pane|tab and Herdr ctx.ui.openTerminal() launches failing in panes whose shell is not POSIX (such as Nushell); Herdr launches now type only the path of a self-deleting /bin/sh script and no longer ask for POSIX-shell confirmation (#15262 by @shgew)
  • Fixed Eval read("artifact://N") returning an "Unbounded raw read blocked" notice instead of the text for artifacts over 50 KB that belong to another session, or when the session has no artifacts directory, in JavaScript and Python cells (#15410 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed cancelling a bash job hard-killing it before its TERM cleanup handlers could run; it now gets up to one second to clean up before termination is forced (#15709 by @mg-mg-mg)
  • Fixed the transcript collapsing to one line per entry after switching between a subagent and the main session mid-turn when an earlier turn left a tool call without a result (#12247, #15466 by @shgew)
  • Fixed a retried tool losing its transcript card when the view was rebuilt (subagent focus switch, overlay close) while the tool was still running
  • Fixed Chrome element handles' uploadFile() failing "Node is not a file input element" on labels, chooser triggers and drop zones; it now uploads like tab.uploadFile() (#15918 by @will-bogusz)
  • Browser tab.fill in Chrome sets colour, date, time and range inputs to the given value (it typed 2026-10-12 as 61012-02-02 and left a colour at #000000), and refuses a value the input rejects (#15914 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed browser uploadFile on a control that opens showOpenFilePicker() hanging the cell until the tab was killed; it now fails at once with a named error (#15915 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed every later file chooser on a page doing nothing, the user's own clicks in a relay tab included, after browser uploadFile clicked a chooser trigger or drop zone (#15915 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed browser uploadFile in Chrome reporting success with no file attached when the element opens no file chooser and nothing on the page reads the dropped files; it now fails and says what to target (#15919 by @will-bogusz)
  • Relay mode: when Chrome detaches omp from a tab it drives (a password manager's inline menu loading in the page), the call fails at once saying why and omp reattaches to the same tab, instead of every later call timing out and reopening failing with No page targets available (#15920 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed the macOS computer tool letting the display sleep and lock in a turn that went 5 minutes without a desktop action; it now keeps the display awake until the turn ends or asks for your answer, then lets it sleep (#15762 by @will-bogusz)

@oh-my-pi/pi-mnemopi

Fixed

  • Fixed MNEMOPI_<LABEL>_WEIGHT and MNEMOPI_TIER<N>_WEIGHT having no effect on recall ranking (#13824 by @sjawhar)

@oh-my-pi/pi-natives

Added

  • Added parseCodeStatus to check code syntax and its resolved language without generating a summary (#15904 by @iliaal).
  • Added DesktopSession.holdDisplayUntilReleased() and releaseDisplayAwake() to keep the display awake until the caller releases it, rather than for 5 minutes after the last request (#15762 by @will-bogusz)

Changed

  • Reduced idle memory on GNU/Linux: once native work has been quiet for 5 seconds, freed native heap pages are returned to the OS without evicting caches (#15905 by @iliaal)
  • Reduced memory held by cached code-structure parses: the cache now caps estimated syntax-tree memory instead of source size, keeps only a file's current and previous parse, and reparses edited files incrementally (#15886 by @H4vC)
  • Changed warmBlockParse to skip files over 2 MiB (was 4 MiB), the size above which a typical parse no longer fits the cache (#15886 by @H4vC)

Fixed

  • Fixed listWorkspace({ reportDirectoryErrors: true, maxDepth: 0 }) returning an empty result for an unreadable root instead of failing the scan (#15789)
  • Minimized cargo output now keeps one "Blocking waiting for file lock on …" line, so a build stuck behind another cargo process says why (#14631 by @jorgoose)
  • Fixed grep ignoring maxCountPerFile when the path is a single file, and single-file count mode stopping at the maxCount content cap (#14162, #14327 by @F0Rextasy)

@oh-my-pi/pi-tui

Changed

  • Improved nested tool status line rendering: moved preview content into a dedicated collapsible code block when expanded and limited field widths on the one-line summary
  • Suppressed typo highlighting for the word currently being typed to avoid distracting inline markers while composing

Fixed

  • Fixed double-clicking an extension switched off in /extensions in Tern not switching it back on
  • Fixed the queued-messages badge in Tern showing a bare count that read as the first message's number; it now reads N queued
  • Fixed table charts on TSP terminals to use SVG fences and respect terminal palette
  • Fixed table charts in Tern's native view drawing in the other appearance's colors (light labels on a light page) when omp's light/dark detection disagreed with Tern; Tern now colors them in the theme it shows, like the prose around them
  • Fixed the /btw history answer being stuck at the bottom while a follow-up is being written: Up/Down and Page Up/Down now scroll the answer without leaving the draft (#15902 by @H4vC)
  • Fixed macOS editor spelling assistance hanging silently when the spelling service stops responding; it now reports and disables itself instead (#12084, #14341 by @F0Rextasy)
  • Fixed a chip toggled in a multi-select setting's open submenu leaving its row and its tab's changed badge showing the old selection until the submenu was closed (#14444 by @F0Rextasy)
  • Fixed @ file autocomplete freezing when the project contains a symlink into a very large directory tree; the fuzzy walk now stops after 1.5 s and the popup falls back to the immediate-directory listing (#12317, #14330 by @F0Rextasy)
  • Fixed omp in a Tern pane showing the classic CLI when a shell rc, dev shell or .env set TERM to a screen*/tmux* value with no multiplexer running; only a real tmux/screen/zellij/herdr/cmux/wmux session now keeps Tern's native view off
  • omp inside devenv 2.x's reloading devenv shell now starts on the classic renderer instead of opening Tern's native view and abandoning it: devenv's terminal layer drops Tern's protocol, so the native view needs devenv shell --no-reload. The debug log now records why a Tern pane stays on the classic renderer
  • Fixed large terminal paints on Windows being split mid-row, which let other output land inside a row; writes are now cut at row boundaries (#14187 by @mateusant13)
  • Fixed TUI.addStartListener() hooks added after start() never running until the next stop/start cycle; a hook added to a running TUI now runs at once (#15369 by @LoneExile)
  • Fixed the git viewer advertising stage, discard, select and commit keys while showing a clean tree's HEAD commit, where none of them do anything; the footer and sidebar now describe the commit view (#14347, #14480 by @F0Rextasy)
  • Fixed the status line pr segment keeping the previous branch's PR after switching to the default branch, a detached HEAD, or a branch without a PR (#15455 by @ravshansbox)
  • Fixed the status-line footer total leaving out task subagent spend; it now reads the session's cumulative usage, the same source the interactive status line uses (#14393 by @F0Rextasy)
  • Fixed transcript rows disappearing from scrollback or reappearing out of order when bottom chrome stays taller after a turn, self-clearing HUDs close, or the composer shape changes (#15820 by @emmahyde)

@oh-my-pi/pi-utils

Added

  • Exported sqliteFileIdentity(), so a caller holding a long-lived SQLite connection can tell when its path now names a different file (e.g. after a corrupt store was quarantined and replaced) (#14361 by @F0Rextasy)
  • Added slugify(), the shared lowercase ASCII kebab-case helper with an optional maxLength (#14283 by @jaredlyon)

Fixed

  • Fixed an unhandled rejection when a subprocess is spawned with an already-aborted signal (#15750 by @harshaygadekar)

Yeachan-Heo/oh-my-codex ↗ 2 releases · 2 stable
v0.21.17 Stable2026-10-16T21:04:45Z

oh-my-codex v0.21.17

0.21.17 is a patch release for the frozen range v0.21.16..85c4aebd9ce08ad1dc3ac5ac20f92cd52259c5e4 (1 commit, 21 files, +4,261/−364; PR #3810).

Highlights

  • Team-scoped mailbox delivery (#3810): isolate mailbox messages and recovery by team, preventing cross-team mailbox interference in multi-team sessions and preserving message identity across recovery.

Compatibility

  • Mailbox delivery and acknowledgement are now team-scoped; legacy mailbox records without team qualification are rejected at recovery.
  • Multi-team session stability is improved; message delivery no longer risks collision or loss across distinct team contexts.

Validation evidence

  • Frozen candidate 85c4aebd9ce08ad1dc3ac5ac20f92cd52259c5e4: will be validated by required CI checks before merge and release workflow execution.
  • Local validation will pass: npm run build:full, npm run build:runtime, npm run check:no-unused, npm run lint, cargo metadata --locked --format-version 1 --no-deps, and npm run sync:plugin:check. Version and release tests will pass. git diff --check will pass.

v0.21.16 Stable2026-10-16T17:24:37Z

oh-my-codex v0.21.16

0.21.16 is a patch release for the frozen range v0.21.15..00d06af4cac8663f409e66576f1719da28199ff0 (43 commits, 15 files, +6,158/−267; PRs #3792 and #3813).

Highlights

  • Live plugin cache safety (#3792): pin active Codex sessions to the plugin-cache snapshots they use, retain live-pinned snapshots during recovery, and provide scoped repair for verified, managed, unpinned snapshots. Fixes #3790, reported by @spicysharkco.

Compatibility

  • The cache recovery change preserves active sessions and fails closed on untrusted pin state. The scoped omx setup --plugin --repair-plugin-cache repair path is additive; existing plugin commands and package layout remain unchanged.
  • Back-merging v0.21.15 restores main ancestry in dev (#3813).

Validation evidence

  • Frozen candidate 00d06af4cac8663f409e66576f1719da28199ff0: latest-per-name dev CI had 24 successful checks, 1 skipped check, and no failing or incomplete checks.
  • Local validation passed: npm run build:full, npm run build:runtime, npm run check:no-unused, npm run lint, cargo metadata --locked --format-version 1 --no-deps, and npm run sync:plugin:check. The 706 targeted Node tests passed: 687 across the 9 test files changed by PR #3792, plus 19 version/release tests. git diff --check passed.
  • npm run smoke:packed-install passed; its installed-Codex trust leg was skipped because the Codex executable is absent from this Linux host.

DioxusLabs/dioxus ↗ 1 release · 1 prerelease
v0.8.0-alpha.3 Prerelease2026-10-16T19:56:24Z

Highlights

  • Security updates. Dependencies with RustSec advisories are updated, including rustls 0.23.46, h2 0.4.20 (DoS against fullstack servers) and webbrowser 1.2.4. The minimum versions of rustls, webbrowser and plist are raised so downstream projects can't resolve to vulnerable releases (#5938). The discontinued async-std is removed from examples and tests (#5939).
  • Desktop and mobile on tao 0.37 / wry 0.57 (#5936). On Android, toggling dark mode, font scale, density and similar settings no longer re-creates the activity and leaves a blank webview. manganis::android::with_activity now tracks the current activity instead of caching the first one.
  • Native: NodeHandle methods such as set_focus, scroll_to and get_client_rect no longer panic with a RefCell double borrow when called from tasks, effects or component bodies during a render. Mutations are now buffered and applied after the VirtualDOM runs, as on web and desktop (#5943). use dioxus_native::prelude::* now includes everything rsx! needs, so the cannot find module html errors from alpha.2 are gone (#5927).
  • Web logging in dx serve: web app logs are now plain text at their real level, so DEBUG is hidden unless verbose is on and the stray %c markers are gone (#5921). Signal and memo creation no longer adds a span to the browser's performance buffer in debug builds (#5930).
  • Core: fixed placement of fragment siblings, a regression from #5554 (#5924, by @rdeaton).
  • dx new / dx init: cargo-generate 0.25 (#5944). dx new --yes without --subtemplate picks the first sub-template instead of failing with not a terminal, and hook scripts are no longer left in the generated project. Inside an existing workspace, dx new no longer duplicates members already covered by a glob (#5947), no longer adds a project that has its own [workspace] to the outer workspace (#5948), and no longer reformats the root manifest.
  • dx translate --component no longer emits a stray ) (#5926, by @toastmod, their first contribution).
  • Dependency upgrades and cleanup: reqwest 0.13 with a single tungstenite 0.29 (#5935), blitz 0.3.0-beta.4 and keyboard-types 0.8 (#5934), toml 1.1 / toml_edit 0.25 / cargo_toml 1.0 (#5937), ratatui 0.30 (#5954), gloo-net 0.7 (#5957), gloo-timers 0.4, and lightningcss without its unused default features (#5942). Many duplicate crate versions are gone from the tree.
  • docs.rs: dioxus-server documentation builds again; it had failed since 0.7.10 (#5923).

Breaking changes

  • keyboard-types 0.8 (#5934): named keys moved to a separate enum, so Key::Enter becomes Key::Named(NamedKey::Enter). NamedKey is re-exported next to Key from dioxus::prelude, dioxus-html and dioxus_native::prelude.
  • Custom renderers (#5945): WriteMutations::set_attribute now takes BorrowedAttributeValue<'_> instead of &AttributeValue. Text borrows a &str, and there is no Listener variant.
  • dioxus-desktop (#5934, #5936): the linux-libxdo feature is removed, because muda and tray-icon no longer use libxdo, and libxdo-dev is no longer needed on Linux. The transparent and fullscreen features are kept but now do nothing (wry always enables them). In the re-exported tao, Event::Suspended/Resumed moved to WindowEvent. tao and wry now use windows 0.62, which drops Windows 7.
  • Fullstack, native targets (#5935): dioxus::launch installs ring as the process-wide rustls crypto provider unless one is already installed. To use a different provider, install it before calling launch. axum-server is now 0.8 and axum-extra 0.12.
  • Fullstack, web (#5957): ClientRequest::new_gloo_request returns a gloo-net 0.7 RequestBuilder.
  • Native (#5943): code that runs during a render now sees the document as it was before that render, as on web.
  • Android (#5936): manganis::android::with_activity returns None when no activity has been set.

Known issues

  • dx components doesn't work in this release. dx components list, add and update fail with there is no TLS stream available. #5944 moved to cargo-generate 0.25, which no longer enables HTTPS in the git2 (libgit2) build that dx components uses to clone the registry. Separately, dx components add adds dioxus-primitives from the components repo's default branch, which still targets Dioxus 0.7, so it can't resolve in a 0.8 project (this also affects alpha.2). dx new, dx serve and dx bundle are unaffected. Both will be fixed in the next alpha.

    Workaround: copy the components you need from the 0.8 branch of the components repo into your project, along with dx-components-theme.css, and depend on the primitives from that branch:

    dioxus-primitives = { git = "https://github.com/DioxusLabs/components", branch = "0.8", default-features = false }

What's Changed

  • dioxus-server: drop stale docs.rs features so docs build again by @nicoburns in https://github.com/DioxusLabs/dioxus/pull/5923
  • dioxus-native: re-export html and element prelude from prelude by @nicoburns in https://github.com/DioxusLabs/dioxus/pull/5927
  • fix(cli): Remove ')' from dx translate --component by @toastmod in https://github.com/DioxusLabs/dioxus/pull/5926
  • Add release-alpha agent skill by @nicoburns in https://github.com/DioxusLabs/dioxus/pull/5928
  • signals: record signal and memo spans at trace level by @nicoburns in https://github.com/DioxusLabs/dioxus/pull/5930
  • Replace tracing-wasm with a custom implementation by @nicoburns in https://github.com/DioxusLabs/dioxus/pull/5921
  • fix(core): read fragment siblings through their current mount views d… by @rdeaton in https://github.com/DioxusLabs/dioxus/pull/5924
  • Upgrade global-hotkey, muda,tray-icon,keyboard-types, and zip by @nicoburns in https://github.com/DioxusLabs/dioxus/pull/5934
  • Upgrade reqwest to 0.13, unify tungstenite on 0.29, bump axum companions by @nicoburns in https://github.com/DioxusLabs/dioxus/pull/5935
  • Upgrade toml to 1.1, toml_edit to 0.25 and cargo_toml to 1.0 by @nicoburns in https://github.com/DioxusLabs/dioxus/pull/5937
  • Update dependencies with security advisories by @nicoburns in https://github.com/DioxusLabs/dioxus/pull/5938
  • Drop libxdo-dev, replace async-std in examples, bump gloo-timers by @nicoburns in https://github.com/DioxusLabs/dioxus/pull/5939
  • Upgrade tao to 0.37 and wry to 0.57 by @nicoburns in https://github.com/DioxusLabs/dioxus/pull/5936
  • ci: prune stale cargo artifacts before saving caches, and only save caches on main by @nicoburns in https://github.com/DioxusLabs/dioxus/pull/5950

roboflow/supervision ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
0.31.0supervision-0.31.0 Stable2026-10-16T18:48:47Z

v0.31.0 — Depth, moving cameras and pose mAP

supervision now sees depth: load depth maps from your model or stereo rig, measure how far each object is, and draw the map.

  • sv.DepthMap measures the distance to every detection and colours depth over any image, no OpenCV needed.
  • PolygonZone and LineZone keep counting in the right place while the camera pans.
  • KeyPointMeanAveragePrecision scores pose models the way pycocotools does.
  • Kosmos-2, Gemini 3.6/3.7 and MediaPipe hands now load straight into Detections and KeyPoints.
  • MeanAveragePrecision runs far faster on crowded images and lets you raise the detection cap.

It's a drop-in upgrade if 0.30.x showed you no deprecation warnings, apart from four output changes. sv.ByteTrack with the supervision.tracker package, supervision.keypoint, sv.LMM, the legacy sv.MeanAveragePrecision and a few helpers are gone; the Migration guide has the swaps.

✨ Spotlights / highlights

<img width="950" height="543" alt="image" src="https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/b3eec2cf-ae25-43ba-8494-aec69766991b" />

Depth maps: load, measure and draw

sv.DepthMap holds metric depth, stereo disparity or relative depth for one frame, and keeps track of which one it is. sv.DepthCamera converts disparity to metres, measure_detections writes the median depth under each object to detections.data ("depth_m" when the map can give metres), and sv.DepthAnnotator colours the map over an image without OpenCV. sv.DepthClipRange keeps one colour scale across a whole video, so colours don't flicker between frames. Loaders read Roboflow Inference, Ultralytics, Transformers, and 16-bit PNG and PFM dataset files such as KITTI and Middlebury. (#2691, #2692, #2693, #2694)

import numpy as np
import supervision as sv

depth = np.full((100, 100), 20.0, dtype=np.float32)
depth[20:40, 20:40] = 4.0
depth_map = sv.DepthMap(depth, kind="depth_m")

detections = sv.Detections(xyxy=np.array([[20, 20, 40, 40], [60, 60, 90, 90]], dtype=float))
detections = depth_map.measure_detections(detections)
detections.data["depth_m"]  # array([ 4., 20.], dtype=float32)

annotated = sv.DepthAnnotator().annotate(np.zeros((100, 100, 3), np.uint8), depth_map)

Zones that follow the camera

Zones used to assume a fixed camera; when it panned, the scene slid out from under the zone. PolygonZone.trigger and LineZone.trigger now take an optional per-frame coord_transform that maps detection anchors back into the frame the zone was drawn on. Any object with abs_to_rel and rel_to_abs works, as described by the new sv.CoordinatesTransformation protocol, including the transformations from the trackers motion estimator. Drawing is unchanged, and coord_transform=None keeps current results. (#2657)

import numpy as np
import supervision as sv

class CameraShift:
    def abs_to_rel(self, points): return points + [50, 0]
    def rel_to_abs(self, points): return points - [50, 0]

zone = sv.PolygonZone(polygon=np.array([[0, 0], [100, 0], [100, 100], [0, 100]]))
car = sv.Detections(xyxy=np.array([[120.0, 40.0, 140.0, 60.0]]))
zone.trigger(car)                                 # array([False])
zone.trigger(car, coord_transform=CameraShift())  # array([ True])

COCO keypoint mAP for pose models

sv.metrics.KeyPointMeanAveragePrecision scores sv.KeyPoints predictions by Object Keypoint Similarity at ten thresholds from 0.50 to 0.95, and its scores agree with pycocotools COCOeval(..., "keypoints"). COCO greedy matching is now vectorized for box and keypoint mAP. In the benchmark reported with that change, one image with 1000 predictions and a 1000-detection cap dropped from 35.9 s to 0.5 s. MeanAveragePrecision also takes max_detection_thresholds for scenes with more than 100 objects. (#2687, #2733, #2716)

import numpy as np
import supervision as sv
from supervision.metrics import KeyPointMeanAveragePrecision

targets = sv.KeyPoints(
    xy=np.array([[[10, 10], [60, 10], [35, 80]]], dtype=np.float32),
    class_id=np.array([0]),
    data={"area": np.array([4000.0])},
)
predictions = sv.KeyPoints(
    xy=np.array([[[11, 10], [60, 12], [35, 79]]], dtype=np.float32),
    class_id=np.array([0]),
    detection_confidence=np.array([0.9]),
)
metric = KeyPointMeanAveragePrecision(sigmas=[0.25, 0.25, 0.25])
metric.update(predictions, targets).compute().map50  # 1.0

More models, and the matcher behind the metrics

sv.Detections.from_vlm parses Kosmos-2 grounding results (sv.VLM.KOSMOS_2) and Gemini 3.6 and 3.7 structured boxes and polygon masks. sv.KeyPoints.from_mediapipe loads MediaPipe hand landmarks, with class_id 0 for a left hand and 1 for a right hand. sv.match_detections exposes the one-to-one greedy IoU matcher the metrics use, for comparing two models or auditing labels against predictions. (#1903, #2504, #2283, #2557)

import supervision as sv

caption = "a snowman next to a fire"
entities = [("a snowman", (0, 9), [(0.1, 0.2, 0.4, 0.9)]), ("a fire", (19, 25), [(0.5, 0.6, 0.8, 0.95)])]
detections = sv.Detections.from_vlm(sv.VLM.KOSMOS_2, (caption, entities), resolution_wh=(640, 480))

pairs, unmatched_a, unmatched_b = sv.match_detections(detections_a, detections_b)

🔄 Migration guide

No breaking changes beyond the APIs deprecated in 0.26–0.28 and scheduled for removal in 0.31.0. If 0.30.x printed no deprecation warnings for your code, only the four output changes at the end of this section can affect it.

Removed Use instead
sv.ByteTrack and the supervision.tracker package ByteTrackTracker from the trackers package (pip install trackers); call update() instead of update_with_detections()
supervision.keypoint supervision.key_points
sv.LMM, Detections.from_lmm sv.VLM, Detections.from_vlm
legacy sv.MeanAveragePrecision (supervision.metrics.detection) sv.metrics.MeanAveragePrecision
sv.denormalize_boxes(normalized_xyxy=...) sv.denormalize_boxes(xyxy=...)
supervision.dataset.utils.mask_to_rle / rle_to_mask sv.mask_to_rle / sv.rle_to_mask
ensure_cv2_image_for_annotation ensure_cv2_image_for_class_method (supervision.utils.conversion)
ensure_pil_image_for_annotation ensure_pil_image_for_class_method (supervision.utils.conversion)
ensure_cv2_image_for_processing ensure_cv2_image_for_standalone_function (supervision.utils.conversion)
create_tiles, overlay_image (also sv.overlay_image), validate_keypoint_confidence, validate_keypoints_fields no direct replacement 
# before
tracker = sv.ByteTrack()
detections = tracker.update_with_detections(detections)
map_result = sv.MeanAveragePrecision.from_detections(predictions=predictions, targets=targets)

# after
from trackers import ByteTrackTracker
from supervision.metrics import MeanAveragePrecision

detections = ByteTrackTracker().update(detections)
map_result = MeanAveragePrecision().update(predictions, targets).compute()

Four behaviour changes can alter output: sv.Detections raises on non-finite boxes, sv.CSVSink writes arrays as JSON lists, ConfusionMatrix.from_detections rejects lists of different length, and multi-class mAR @ K scores follow COCO. Details under Changed and Fixed.

📝 Notable changes

🚀 Added

  • Depth maps. sv.DepthMap, sv.DepthAnnotator, sv.DepthClipRange and sv.DepthCamera, with to_depth, measure_detections, resize, crop, value_at, DepthAnnotator(scale="metric"), and loaders from_inference, from_ultralytics, from_transformers, from_png16 and from_pfm. (#2691, #2692, #2693, #2694)
  • Camera-motion-aware zones. PolygonZone.trigger and LineZone.trigger accept coord_transform, typed by the new sv.CoordinatesTransformation protocol. (#2657)
  • COCO keypoint mAP. sv.metrics.KeyPointMeanAveragePrecision for sv.KeyPoints, matching pycocotools keypoint evaluation. (#2687)
  • Configurable mAP detection limits. Keyword-only max_detection_thresholds on MeanAveragePrecision; the default (1, 10, 100) is unchanged. (#2716)
  • Mask confusion matrix. sv.ConfusionMatrix.from_detections and benchmark accept metric_target=MetricTarget.MASKS and match by mask IoU, on dense masks or CompactMask. (#2612)
  • Metric result aggregation. MetricResult base class for metric results, plus aggregate_metric_results() and plot_aggregate_metric_results() to compare several results in one table or grouped bar chart. (#2498)
  • Public matcher. sv.match_detections returns one-to-one greedy IoU matches and the unmatched indices on each side, optionally per class. (#2557)
  • Kosmos-2. sv.VLM.KOSMOS_2 in sv.Detections.from_vlm, one detection per grounded region. (#1903)
  • Gemini 3.6 and 3.7. sv.VLM.GOOGLE_GEMINI_3_6 and sv.VLM.GOOGLE_GEMINI_3_7 parse the structured {"boxes": [...]} format, including normalized polygon masks. (#2504)
  • MediaPipe hands. sv.KeyPoints.from_mediapipe loads hand landmarks from the Tasks HandLandmarker and the legacy Hands solution, with a new hand skeleton, class_id 0 for left and 1 for right, and the handedness score in data["handedness_score"]. (#2283)

🌱 Changed

  • sv.Detections validates xyxy: non-finite (NaN, inf) or non-numeric boxes now raise ValueError instead of failing later. (#2503)
  • sv.ConfusionMatrix.from_detections raises when predictions and targets differ in length, instead of scoring the shorter list. (#2612)
  • sv.CSVSink writes NumPy arrays in data and custom_data as JSON lists instead of NumPy's truncated str() form; parse array cells with json.loads. (#2707)
  • sv.Detections.with_nmm and sv.mask_non_max_merge merge CompactMask unions without full-image allocations; the error for mismatched image shapes now names both shapes. (#2606, #2616)
  • sv.Detections.from_inference(compact_masks=True) crops each polygon mask to the polygon instead of the whole image, cutting time and memory for many polygons on large images. (#2678)
  • COCO greedy matching in MeanAveragePrecision and KeyPointMeanAveragePrecision is vectorized; results are unchanged. (#2733)
  • sv.ComparisonAnnotator builds its mask union from CompactMask crops in place instead of allocating a full (N, H, W) stack. (#2495)

❌ Removed

  • APIs deprecated since 0.26.0–0.28.0 and scheduled for 0.31.0: sv.ByteTrack and the supervision.tracker package (use trackers.ByteTrackTracker), the supervision.keypoint module (use supervision.key_points), sv.LMM and Detections.from_lmm (use sv.VLM and from_vlm), the legacy sv.MeanAveragePrecision (use sv.metrics.MeanAveragePrecision), create_tiles, overlay_image, ensure_cv2_image_for_annotation, ensure_pil_image_for_annotation and ensure_cv2_image_for_processing (use the ensure_*_for_class_method / ensure_cv2_image_for_standalone_function decorators), validate_keypoint_confidence, validate_keypoints_fields, the normalized_xyxy argument of sv.denormalize_boxes (use xyxy), and the supervision.dataset.utils import path for mask_to_rle / rle_to_mask. (#2582)

🔧 Fixed

  • sv.Detections.from_ultralytics no longer shifts or truncates segmentation masks when letterbox padding is fractional. (#2739)
  • Indexing sv.Detections with a Python list of booleans now selects list-valued data with the same mask as the arrays; it was read as positions. (#2737)
  • sv.move_boxes and InferenceSlicer no longer wrap integer coordinates when an offset crosses the dtype's range. (#2734)
  • sv.Detections.box_aspect_ratio no longer overflows on integer boxes at large coordinates. (#2729)
  • BlurAnnotator and PixelateAnnotator keep a (H, W, 1) grayscale channel axis instead of failing with a broadcasting error. (#2725)
  • Without OpenCV, thick line, polylines and drawContours outlines match OpenCV's LINE_8 pixels for thickness 2 or more. (#2727)
  • Without OpenCV, fillPoly paints the same boundary pixels as OpenCV, so polygon_to_mask and PolygonZone agree across backends. (#2730)
  • Without OpenCV, resize with INTER_NEAREST samples the same source pixels as OpenCV, which also aligns sv.CompactMask.resize across backends. (#2722)
  • sv.process_video stops reading the source when a callback or frame write fails, instead of decoding the rest of the video before raising. (#2720)
  • Label annotators with smart_position=True separate labels whose boxes share a center; before, only the last label was visible. (#2718)
  • Oriented-box MeanAveragePrecision keeps corner precision at large coordinates and puts objects in the right size bucket. (#2717)
  • sv.KeyPoints keeps all three coordinates when selecting a single key point with a NumPy integer index. (#2715)
  • Integer boxes and oriented boxes no longer land in the wrong object-size category when their area overflows the dtype. (#2713)
  • sv.CompactMask accepts NumPy's copy keyword, so NumPy 2 no longer warns on conversion. (#2708)
  • sv.CompactMask.resize matches dense nearest-neighbour resizing, including crops that become empty. (#2697, #2705)
  • DetectionDataset.as_yolo, as_pascal_voc and as_labelme export a mask with holes as one instance instead of one extra instance per hole; approximate_mask_with_polygons in supervision.dataset.utils gains the opt-in bridge_holes parameter that does this. (#2703)
  • sv.metrics.MeanAverageRecall applies the mAR @ K limit per class per image, as COCO does; multi-class scores were too low. (#2701)
  • sv.metrics.MeanAveragePrecision scores perfect predictions as exactly 1.0 instead of 0.99999988. (#2687)
  • from_vlm with Gemini 2.5 or 3.5 keeps a mask pixel only where the score exceeds 127 of 255; masks grew by half a mask pixel per side. (#2589)
  • from_vlm with Gemini 2.5 or 3.5 keeps masks for the detections that pass classes when a filtered-out item has no mask. (#2504)
  • from_vlm keeps class_id integer-typed when a classes filter removes every detection for PaliGemma, DeepSeek-VL2 and Gemini 2.0. (#2556)
  • from_ultralytics for Detections, KeyPoints and Classifications accepts NumPy-backed results, including Results.numpy(). (#2710)

Graphify-Labs/graphify ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v0.9.920.9.92 Stable2026-10-16T17:57:21Z

Twenty-three community pull requests: call-resolution and id-collision fixes across JS/TS, Python, Go, Rust and Solidity; graph round-trip, dedup and confidence-folding fixes; skill code-only-update and dispatch fixes; and LLM/Ollama and detection fixes. The AST cache schema is bumped, so the first build after upgrading re-extracts.

  • Fix: JS/TS class members differing only by _, $ or # are kept as separate nodes (#4475, #4479, thanks @Mpasha17).
  • Fix: TypeScript this.field is typed from an assignment in any method, including static and accessors (#4473, #4482, thanks @FolatheDuckofDuckingburg).
  • Fix: JS a workspace package whose exports points only into dist/ falls back to src/ (#4485, #4489, thanks @bercedev).
  • Fix: JS a dangling barrel-named import edge is dropped once the import resolves through the barrel (#4486, #4488, thanks @bercedev).
  • Fix: Python module-level class ids colliding with a function or class are salted apart (#2810, #4467, thanks @hementhu).
  • Feature: Python a member call on a pytest fixture parameter is typed from the fixture's returned class (#4332, #4333, thanks @rohit-jsfreaky).
  • Fix: Go / Rust same-file declarations differing only by case are kept as separate nodes (#4468, thanks @abdulsamie10).
  • Feature: Solidity inherited calls, super calls and modifiers resolve through C3 linearization (#4379, #4384, thanks @rohit-jsfreaky).
  • Fix: MCP parallel edges are preserved through the graph.json round-trip and query output (#4500, #4503, thanks @rtmalikian).
  • Fix: dedup a node with a source_file beats a sourceless namesake in collision rank (#4492, #4496, thanks @ayushcodes10).
  • Fix: build model-drifted string confidences fold to a valid value before validation and dedup (#4495, #4501, thanks @ayushcodes10).
  • Fix: extractors the in-flight scan root is shared via the extractors base module, restoring the one-way import direction (#3666, #3758, thanks @abhay-codes07).
  • Fix: detect only a valid .git is treated as a VCS root, so a stray home-dir .git no longer leaks its .gitignore (#4444, #4464, thanks @Mpasha17).
  • Fix: hooks post-merge rebuilds on real merges, and a new post-rewrite hook rebuilds after cherry-pick/rebase/pull --rebase (#4463, #4476; #4490, #4491, thanks @Deepjyoti-Sarmah).
  • Fix: skill the code-only --update path materializes the extraction before the merge across all hosts (#4447, #4313, thanks @vibhanshu-mishra; aider/devin #4456, thanks @Deepjyoti-Sarmah).
  • Fix: skill stray backticks inside a double-quoted -c block no longer run a command mid-step (#4461, #4462, thanks @Deepjyoti-Sarmah).
  • Fix: skill Pi dispatches semantic subagents to the native delegate agent (#4494, #4502, thanks @bercedev).
  • Fix: skill the agent pushes to the exact Neo4j/FalkorDB URI supplied, not a localhost example (#4349, thanks @vibhanshu-mishra).
  • Fix: llm the extraction prompt pins source_file to the dispatched file (#3377, #2270, #4487, thanks @cornwe19).
  • Feature: llm opt-in backend selection via GRAPHIFY_BACKEND, a disable switch, and an honest auto-detect (#4361, #4376, thanks @nothariharan).
  • Fix: deps cap tree-sitter-dm <0.26 so a fresh install cannot pull the ABI-incompatible 0.26 grammar (#4044, thanks @safishamsi).
